SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[CertificationID], [a].[CreatedBy], [a].[CreatedDate], [a].[ExamID], [a].[Remarks], [a].[StatusID], [a].[SubmittedDate], [a].[UpdatedBy], [a].[UpdatedDate], [a].[UserID], [c].[Id], [c].[CreatedBy], [c].[CreatedDate], [c].[Description], [c].[EntityID], [c].[EntityTypeID], [c].[IsActive], [c].[Name], [c].[ObtainedYear], [c].[UpdatedBy], [c].[UpdatedDate], [s].[Id], [s].[CreatedBy], [s].[CreatedDate], [s].[StatusName], [s].[UpdatedBy], [s].[UpdatedDate], [e].[Id], [e].[CreatedBy], [e].[CreatedDate], [e].[ExamCenter], [e].[ExamName], [e].[ExamSlot], [e].[UpdatedBy], [e].[UpdatedDate], [u].[Id], [u].[AddressID], [u].[CreatedBy], [u].[CreatedDate], [u].[Email], [u].[FirstName], [u].[LastName], [u].[PasswordHash], [u].[RoleID], [u].[StatusID], [u].[UpdatedBy], [u].[UpdatedDate]
FROM [Applications] AS [a]
INNER JOIN [Certifications] AS [c] ON [a].[CertificationID] = [c].[Id]
INNER JOIN [Statuses] AS [s] ON [a].[StatusID] = [s].[Id]
